{
  "path": "~/code/rust-editor/src/tracing.rs",
  "content": "//! Debug tracing infrastructure for development diagnostics\n//!\n//! Provides structured logging with scoped filtering for debugging\n//! multi-cursor, selection, and state transition issues.\n//!\n//! # Usage\n//!\n//! Configure via RUST_LOG environment variable:\n//! - `RUST_LOG=debug` - all debug logs\n//! - `RUST_LOG=cursor=trace,selection=debug` - scoped filtering\n//! - `RUST_LOG=token::update=debug` - module-level filtering\n\nuse tracing_subscriber::{fmt, prelude::*, EnvFilter};\n\nuse crate::model::EditorState;\n\n/// Initialize tracing subscriber\n///\n/// Respects RUST_LOG env var for filtering:\n/// - `RUST_LOG=debug` - all debug logs\n/// - `RUST_LOG=cursor=trace,selection=debug` - scoped filtering\n/// - `RUST_LOG=token::update::editor=debug` - module-level filtering\npub fn init() {\n    let filter = EnvFilter::try_from_default_env().unwrap_or_else(|_| EnvFilter::new(\"warn\"));\n\n    tracing_subscriber::registry()\n        .with(fmt::layer().with_target(true).with_line_number(true))\n        .with(filter)\n        .init();\n}\n\n/// Lightweight snapshot of cursor/selection state for diffing\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct CursorSnapshot {\n    pub cursor_count: usize,\n    pub active_idx: usize,\n    pub cursors: Vec<CursorInfo>,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct CursorInfo {\n    pub line: usize,\n    pub column: usize,\n    pub anchor: (usize, usize),\n    pub head: (usize, usize),\n    pub selection_empty: bool,\n}\n\nimpl CursorSnapshot {\n    pub fn from_editor(editor: &EditorState) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            cursor_count: editor.cursors.len(),\n            active_idx: editor.active_cursor_index,\n            cursors: editor\n                .cursors\n                .iter()\n                .zip(&editor.selections)\n                .map(|(c, s)| CursorInfo {\n                    line: c.line,\n                    column: c.column,\n                    anchor: (s.anchor.line, s.anchor.column),\n                    head: (s.head.line, s.head.column),\n                    selection_empty: s.is_empty(),\n                })\n                .collect(),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Generate a diff description between two snapshots\n    pub fn diff(&self, other: &CursorSnapshot) -> Option<String> {\n        if self.cursor_count != other.cursor_count {\n            return Some(format!(\n                \"cursor count: {} → {}\",\n                self.cursor_count, other.cursor_count\n            ));\n        }\n\n        let mut changes = Vec::new();\n        for (i, (before, after)) in self.cursors.iter().zip(&other.cursors).enumerate() {\n            if before.line != after.line || before.column != after.column {\n                changes.push(format!(\n                    \"#{}: ({},{}) → ({},{})\",\n                    i, before.line, before.column, after.line, after.column\n                ));\n            }\n            if before.selection_empty != after.selection_empty {\n                let status = if after.selection_empty {\n                    \"cleared\"\n                } else {\n                    \"active\"\n                };\n                changes.push(format!(\"#{}: selection {}\", i, status));\n            }\n        }\n\n        if changes.is_empty() {\n            None\n        } else {\n            Some(changes.join(\"; \"))\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

msg = %msg_name).entered();\n+\n+    let before = model.focused_editor().map(CursorSnapshot::from_editor);\n+\n+    debug!(target: \"message\", msg = %msg_name, \"processing\");\n+\n+    let result = {
  "path": "~/code/rust-editor/src/debug_overlay.rs",
  "content": "//! In-editor debug overlay for real-time state visibility\n//!\n//! Toggle with F8 in debug builds.\n\nuse std::collections::VecDeque;\nuse std::time::Instant;\n\nuse crate::model::{AppModel, EditorState};\n\n/// Maximum number of messages to retain in history\nconst MESSAGE_HISTORY_SIZE: usize = 50;\n\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct DebugOverlay {\n    /// Whether the overlay is visible\n    pub visible: bool,\n    /// Show cursor position details\n    pub show_cursors: bool,\n    /// Show selection ranges\n    pub show_selections: bool,\n    /// Show recent message history\n    pub show_messages: bool,\n    /// Recent message history\n    pub message_history: VecDeque<MessageEntry>,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct MessageEntry {\n    pub timestamp: Instant,\n    pub msg_type: String,\n    pub cursor_diff: Option<String>,\n}\n\nimpl Default for DebugOverlay {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self::new()\n    }\n}\n\nimpl DebugOverlay {\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            visible: false,\n            show_cursors: true,\n            show_selections: true,\n            show_messages: true,\n            message_history: VecDeque::with_capacity(MESSAGE_HISTORY_SIZE),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Toggle overlay visibility\n    pub fn toggle(&mut self) {\n        self.visible = !self.visible;\n    }\n\n    /// Record a message in history\n    pub fn record_message(&mut self, msg_type: String, cursor_diff: Option<String>) {\n        if self.message_history.len() >= MESSAGE_HISTORY_SIZE {\n            self.message_history.pop_front();\n        }\n        self.message_history.push_back(MessageEntry {\n            timestamp: Instant::now(),\n            msg_type,\n            cursor_diff,\n        });\n    }\n\n    /// Generate overlay text lines for rendering\n    pub fn render_lines(&self, model: &AppModel) -> Vec<String> {\n        if !self.visible {\n            return Vec::new();\n        }\n\n        let mut lines = vec![\"─── DEBUG OVERLAY (F8 to hide) ───\".to_string()];\n\n        if let Some(editor) = model.focused_editor() {\n            if self.show_cursors {\n                lines.push(String::new());\n                lines.extend(self.render_cursor_info(editor));\n            }\n\n            if self.show_selections {\n                lines.push(String::new());\n                lines.extend(self.render_selection_info(editor));\n            }\n        }\n\n        if self.show_messages && !self.message_history.is_empty() {\n            lines.push(String::new());\n            lines.push(\"Recent Messages:\".to_string());\n            for entry in self.message_history.iter().rev().take(10) {\n                let age_ms = entry.timestamp.elapsed().as_millis();\n                let diff_str = entry.cursor_diff.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"-\");\n                lines.push(format!(\n                    \"  [{:>4}ms] {} → {}\",\n                    age_ms, entry.msg_type, diff_str\n                ));\n            }\n        }\n\n        lines\n    }\n\n    fn render_cursor_info(&self, editor: &EditorState) -> Vec<String> {\n        let mut lines = vec![format!(\n            \"Cursors: {} (active: #{})\",\n            editor.cursor_count(),\n            editor.active_cursor_index\n        )];\n\n        for (i, cursor) in editor.cursors.iter().enumerate() {\n            let marker = if i == editor.active_cursor_index {\n                \"→\"\n            } else {\n                \" \"\n            };\n            let desired = cursor\n                .desired_column\n                .map(|c| format!(\" (desired: {})\", c))\n                .unwrap_or_default();\n            lines.push(format!(\n                \"  {} #{}: L{}:C{}{}\",\n                marker, i, cursor.line, cursor.column, desired\n            ));\n        }\n\n        lines\n    }\n\n    fn render_selection_info(&self, editor: &EditorState) -> Vec<String> {\n        let mut lines = vec![\"Selections:\".to_string()];\n\n        for (i, sel) in editor.selections.iter().enumerate() {\n            let status = if sel.is_empty() { \"empty\" } else { \"active\" };\n            let reversed = if sel.is_reversed() { \" [rev]\" } else { \"\" };\n            lines.push(format!(\n                \"  #{}: ({},{})→({},{}) [{}]{}\",\n                i,\n                sel.anchor.line,\n                sel.anchor.column,\n                sel.head.line,\n                sel.head.column,\n                status,\n                reversed\n            ));\n        }\n\n        lines\n    }\n}\n"
}

## Assistant

The tests are directly manipulating `primary_cursor_mut()` without updating the corresponding selections. This breaks the invariant that `cursor.to_position() == selection.head`. The tracing instrumentation is now catching this pre-existing invariant violation.

These tests have a bug - when they set the cursor position directly, they need to also update the selection. The instrumentation is working correctly. Let me fix these tests so the tests pass:

## Assistant

I see the issue. On an empty document (with text ""), `line_count()` returns 1 (one empty line), but when the cursor is at line 0, and total_lines is 1, the condition `line + 1 < total_lines` (0+1 < 1 = false) results in selecting to `Position::new(0, 0)` (line 0, column 0) since `line_len` for an empty line is 0.

The issue is that when selecting the whole line, if the document has a second line (e.g., after newline character), we position cursor at the next line's start. But when the empty document has no newline, we keep cursor on line 0.

Wait, looking closer at the error - it says cursor is at (1,0) but selection head is (0,0). This means the cursor position is line 1, but selection says line 0. So something else is happening.

## Assistant

Implementation complete. Here's a summary of what was done:

### Summary

Implemented the tracing instrumentation feature per the design doc:

#### New Files
- [src/tracing.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/tracing.rs) - Tracing setup and `CursorSnapshot` for state diffing
- [src/debug_overlay.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/debug_overlay.rs) - In-editor debug panel (F8 toggle)

#### Modified Files
- **Cargo.toml** - Replaced `log`/`env_logger` with `tracing`/`tracing-subscriber`
- **src/lib.rs** - Added `tracing` and `debug_overlay` modules
- **src/update/mod.rs** - Added `update_traced` wrapper with before/after state diffing
- **src/model/mod.rs** - Added `debug_overlay` field to `AppModel`
- **src/model/editor.rs** - Added `assert_invariants_with_context()` for contextual assertions
- **src/app.rs** - Added F8 hotkey handler
- **src/main.rs** - Changed `env_logger::init()` → `token::tracing::init()`

#### Bug Fixes (found via new invariant checks)
- Fixed `DocumentMsg::Duplicate` not syncing selection after cursor move
- Fixed `DocumentMsg::DeleteLine` not syncing selection after cursor move
- Fixed several test files that were setting cursors without updating selections

#### Usage
```bash
## All debug output
RUST_LOG=debug cargo run

## Cursor operations only
RUST_LOG=cursor=debug cargo run

## Message flow only
RUST_LOG=message=debug cargo run

## Press F8 in editor to toggle debug overlay
```
